10a3(1): bcom ldan 'das kyis yang byang chub sems dpa' chen po rab tu dga' ba'i rgyal po'i dam tshig las byung ba'i sems dpa'i byin gyis brlabs rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting 
10a4(1): nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rab tu dga' ba'i dam tshig ces bya ba de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyi snying po 'di rang gi 
10a5(1): thugs kha nas phyung ngo //badzra sA d+hu//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi thugs las byung ma thag pa 'di ni bcom ldan 'das rdo rje 'dzin pa de nyid de legs so zhes 
10a6(1): bya bar gyur nas/bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi thugs su zhugs te/gcig tu 'dres par gyur nas/rdo rje dgyes pa'i dbyings 
10a7(1): su gnas so//de nas rdo rje dgyes pa'i dbyibs de las 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyi rdul shin tu phra mo dang mnyam pa'i de bzhin gshegs 
10a8(1): de bzhin gshegs pa thams(cad) kyi legs so zhes bya ba lasogs pa dang /sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rdzu 'phrul dang cho 'phrul rnams byas nas rdo rje 
10b1(1): 'dzin ni shin tu dga' bar byed pa dang /rab tu brtan pa'i phyir gcig tu 'dres par gyur nas/byang chub sems dpa' rab tu dga' ba'i rgyal po

11a3(1): rab tu 'jug byed pas//rdo rje thams cad dgyes par byed (lta rdzas dgyes pa spel ba'o////byang chub sems dpa' chen po dang /de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad

208a1(1): @##//can la ni gnod mi bya//de yi srog gi phyir yang ni//'dor bar mi bya gang zhig yin//'tsho ba'i srog gi phyir yang ni/byang chub sems ni don mi bya//rdo rje dril bu'i
